- **lirbox-builder**: Implements one scoped slice of work inside a single lane worktree, against acceptance criteria someone else wrote. Reports observed numbers, never verdicts, and stops at the first red rather than debugging past it. Use as conductor's `lane` (spawned with isolation: worktree) or the implementation lane of a herdr orchestration run. ([liemle3893/lirbox](https://github.com/liemle3893/lirbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/lirbox/agents/lirbox-builder.md) · ★1)
- **lirbox-code-reviewer**: Reviews the changed code on a branch AND fixes what it finds, in one pass. Covers correctness/bugs, security, rule/convention violations, and quality/simplification across severity tiers; resolves every Critical and High finding, keeps the build/lint green, and commits the fixes. Use as a code-quality gate in a delivery workflow. Reports whether the gate passed plus the finding counts. ([liemle3893/lirbox](https://github.com/liemle3893/lirbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/lirbox/agents/lirbox-code-reviewer.md) · ★1)
- **lirbox-docs-writer**: Writes a concise implementation summary of a completed change into docs/changes/ with frontmatter — reading the diff, the goal/ticket, and any per-worker implementation-notes left during the work. Folds design decisions, deviations, tradeoffs, and open questions into one durable record, then commits it. Use as the documentation step of a delivery workflow. ([liemle3893/lirbox](https://github.com/liemle3893/lirbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/lirbox/agents/lirbox-docs-writer.md) · ★1)
- **lirbox-mobile-verifier**: Verifies a MOBILE app change on a simulator/emulator — detects the stack (React Native / Flutter / native iOS / native Android), writes runnable E2E flows (Maestro preferred, Appium fallback) for criteria that can be asserted, and falls back to raw xcrun simctl / adb evidence capture (screenshots, device logs) when no flow runner exists. Honors a frozen engine chain when the caller supplies one. Use as the mobile half of a frontend verification gate in a delivery workflow, or standalone. Simulators/emulators only; distinguishes tooling failure from app failure; never silently passes. ([liemle3893/lirbox](https://github.com/liemle3893/lirbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/lirbox/agents/lirbox-mobile-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **lirbox-planner**: Plans the NEXT ONE slice of an orchestrated run — what it delivers and acceptance criteria stated as commands with expected values — and refuses to enumerate the slice after it. Re-invoked after every slice lands, planning from what actually happened rather than from an original decomposition. Reads code to ground the plan; never implements it. Use as the scoping lane of a herdr orchestration run. ([liemle3893/lirbox](https://github.com/liemle3893/lirbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/lirbox/agents/lirbox-planner.md) · ★1)
- **lirbox-test-writer**: Writes failing tests FIRST (test-driven), from acceptance criteria or a goal, before any implementation exists. Produces runnable tests — tryve E2E YAML and/or unit — that target WHAT the feature does (observable behavior), then confirms they fail for the RIGHT reason. Use as the RED step of a TDD workflow. Never writes implementation code. ([liemle3893/lirbox](https://github.com/liemle3893/lirbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/lirbox/agents/lirbox-test-writer.md) · ★1)
- **lirbox-tryve-enhancer**: Hardens a change's test coverage from the ENGINEERING perspective. Reads the implementation diff and the existing (spec-driven) tests, finds the failure modes acceptance criteria never mention — error paths, boundaries, auth/permission, idempotency, concurrency, limits — and writes the missing tests (tryve E2E YAML and/or unit). Use as a test-hardening gate after the happy path already passes. Never fakes or weakens a pass. ([liemle3893/lirbox](https://github.com/liemle3893/lirbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/lirbox/agents/lirbox-tryve-enhancer.md) · ★1)
- **lirbox-verifier**: Independently verifies a lane's result at a named SHA — re-runs the checks, breaks them on purpose to prove they can fail, and reports quantified pass/fail. Never fixes what it finds. Use as the verification lane of a herdr orchestration run, and as the profile behind the code gate. ([liemle3893/lirbox](https://github.com/liemle3893/lirbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/lirbox/agents/lirbox-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **lirbox-web-verifier**: Verifies a UI-touching WEB change end-to-end — writes runnable E2E specs (Playwright preferred) for criteria that can be asserted, and captures evidence (screenshots, console/network logs, responsive viewport matrix) for criteria that must be judged. Engine-pluggable with graceful fallback (Playwright → browser-MCP → OS-scripted), honoring a frozen engine chain when the caller supplies one. Use as the web half of a frontend verification gate in a delivery workflow, or standalone to verify a web change. Distinguishes tooling failure from app failure; never silently passes. ([liemle3893/lirbox](https://github.com/liemle3893/lirbox/blob/HEAD/plugins/lirbox/agents/lirbox-web-verifier.md) · ★1)

- **litmus-expert**: Specialist for Litmus Edge concepts, terminology, drivers, and API behavior, answered from the live docs.litmus.io resources rather than training data. - **litmus-grounder**: Phase 2 of Litmus, the novel piece. For each atomic claim (especially numeric and prediction atoms), semantically search the web via Claude's inbuilt exa (mcp__claude_ai_Exa_2__*) and produce a citation table classifying the claim as GROUNDED / CONTRADICTED / UNGROUNDED / UNFALSIFIABLE. Uses the bundled Claude exa, not the user's local exa MCP. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-grounder.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-independent-designer**: Phase 1 of Litmus. Receives ONLY problem-statement atoms (NOT the source document) and produces a fresh design for the stated problem. The frame-break: the designer cannot anchor on a document it has never seen. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-independent-designer.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-accountability**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Accountability lens. Identifies who owns this in production, who gets paged, who has authority to roll back, chains of responsibility, oversight gaps, compliance/regulatory accountability, enforceability of stated guarantees (SLOs, SLAs, data-retention promises). Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-accountability.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-cascade**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Cascade lens. Second- and third-order effects, lock-in, vendor capture, copycat propagation (other teams will mimic this), maintenance debt compounding, cognitive-load tax, supply-chain blast radius, downstream-system impact. Time horizons: 0-6 months, 1-3 years, 5-10 years. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-cascade.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-escalation**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Escalation lens. Narrative of worsening failure over time, cracks → amplification → reckoning. Walks the timeline of how this fails (week 2, month 4, month 12, year 2), not the snapshot. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-escalation.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-integrity**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Integrity lens. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-integrity.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-spectrum**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Spectrum lens. Enforced breadth across feasibility / ethics / governance / human-factor / operational-cost axes. Surfaces axes the document did not consider at all. Single-axis review is the failure this lens catches. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-spectrum.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-sycophancy-judge**: Phase 4 of Litmus. Scores each Phase 3 lens output on five behavioral sycophancy signals. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-sycophancy-judge.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-synthesizer**: Phase 5 of Litmus. Reads all prior-phase artifacts and produces report.md per references/report-format.md. No verdict line; evidence-first. - **litreview-updater**: Use to incrementally refresh an EXISTING literature review (from a prior /litreview run) with newly published papers, instead of re-running the full review. Schedulable weekly/monthly (draft + REVIEW REQUIRED when unattended).
